Task Failed ClawBench Trajectory Report
Overview Milestone Funnel Diagnostics Turns (93) Actions (85) Network (306) Persona Metadata & Refs

HelloFresh Veggie Subscription (3 Meals/Wk, 2 People)

Evaluation Result: FAILED (agent_idle) Benchmark: ClawBench Domain: daily_web_tasks / food Model: claude-haiku-4-5-20251001 03_008-daily-life-food-hellofresh-claude-haiku-4-5-20251001-20260325-071107
⚠

Root Failure Cause: Stripe Credit Card Iframe Loading Failure in Sandbox

The agent executed 92 active turns and successfully navigated 8 out of 9 subscription milestones (including Canadian site failover to hellofresh.ca, postal code validation, Veggie meal selection, frequency, account registration, and delivery address). At the final checkout payment step, HelloFresh embedded the Stripe credit card collection form via a cross-origin iframe. In this automated headless browser environment, the iframe failed to render interactive input fields. Despite extensive troubleshooting (F5 page reload, Tab navigation, DOM queries, and injecting JS to force-click the Place Order button), the submit button remained disabled without tokenized payment data. The agent terminated gracefully with a completion summary, and the evaluation harness stopped after 900 seconds of inactivity (stop_reason: agent_idle).

🔍 Technical Diagnostics & Failure Analysis

Why did the evaluation mark this task as FAILED?

In ClawBench web tasks, the evaluator uses an interception schema to detect whether the final target event (in this case, an order placement POST request) was completed. Because HelloFresh delegates credit card tokenization to a third-party Stripe iframe, the checkout form cannot proceed to order completion without valid card tokenization.

Agent Troubleshooting Attempts (Turns 71–92)
  • Waiting & Snapshotting: Paused and snapshotted DOM across multiple turns (Turns 72–75).
  • Page Reload (F5): Triggered browser refresh keypress to reload payment widgets (Turn 80).
  • Keyboard Focus Navigation: Sent Tab key events to focus hidden iframe inputs (Turn 82).
  • DOM Evaluation: Evaluated iframe existence via JS: () => document.querySelectorAll('iframe').length (returned 2 iframes, but cross-origin protected).
  • Forced JS Activation: Ran JS script to override button state: btn.disabled = false; btn.click(); (Turn 90). The form refused submission due to missing payment verification.
Evaluator Interception Outcome
pass: false
stop_reason: "agent_idle"
error: "Session stopped: agent went idle (900s no actions) before triggering the eval interceptor."

After Turn 93, having exhausted all possible browser workarounds, the agent stopped issuing commands and waited. The benchmark runner idle watchdog reached 900 seconds and concluded the session.
